Goose Plugin Configuration Schema\n\n**Current Plugin:** `plugins/goose.toml` (version 1.0)\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\n# Extract project path from first-line metadata's working_dir field\nmethod = \"field\"\nfield = \"working_dir\"\n```\n\n**Data Format:**\n- **Location:** `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl`\n- **Format:** JSONL with first-line session metadata\n- **Line 1:** Session metadata object including `working_dir` field\n- **Subsequent lines:** Message events with `role` and `content[]` blocks\n\n### 2. ProjectDetection::Field Implementation Status\n\n**Current Implementation:** ⚠️ **NOT IMPLEMENTED for JSONL format**\n\nThe `ProjectDetection::Field { field: String }` variant is defined in `src/plugin.rs`:\n```rust\npub enum ProjectDetection {\n    Field { field: String },  // Extract from field in session metadata\n    ParentDir,               // Use parent directory\n    GitRoot,                 // Use git rev-parse\n}\n```\n\n**How it works:**\n- In `src/scraper/mod.rs::detect_project()` (lines 730-771):\n  - `ParentDir`: Returns parent directory of log file\n  - `GitRoot`: Runs `git rev-parse --show-toplevel`\n  - `Field { field: _ }`: Returns `None` with comment: *\"For field-based detection, we need to extract from the first event. This is handled in the parser, return None here\"*\n\n**Problem:** The comment says it's \"handled in the parser\" but jsonl.rs does NOT implement this.\n\n**Evidence from code:**\n1. `src/parser/jsonl.rs` has NO code to extract project from first-line metadata\n2. Line 229 has TODO: `\"Future session metadata accumulation (project, model, version)\"`\n3. Line 765-768 in scraper returns None for Field detection\n4. Only `json_array.rs` implements ProjectDetection::Field (line 177-181)\n\n### 3. How json_array.rs Implements Field Detection (Working Example)\n\nFrom `src/parser/json_array.rs` lines 176-181:\n```rust\nevent.project =\n    if let Some(ProjectDetection::Field { field }) = plugin.parser.project.as_ref() {\n        extract_string(item, field).or_else(|| context.project.clone())\n    } else {\n        context.project.clone()\n    };\n```\n\nThis works because json_array reads each item as an event and can extract fields from it. For jsonl with first-line metadata, the project field is only in line 1, not in every event.\n\n### 4. First-Line Metadata in jsonl.rs\n\n**Current behavior:**\n- First line IS read for session ID extraction (lines 786-850)\n- Envelope unwrapping works for first line (lines 44-129 in `unwrap_envelope()`)\n- First-line metadata can populate `SessionInfo.metadata` (optional Value field)\n- BUT metadata is NOT used for project/model extraction in parsing\n\n**Code evidence:**\n```rust\n// src/parser/jsonl.rs:786-791\nSessionIdSource::Field(field) => {\n    match open_file_maybe_zst(source_path) {\n        Ok(mut reader) => {\n            let mut first_line = String::new();\n            if reader.read_line(&mut first_line).is_ok() {\n                if let Ok(json) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&first_line) {\n                    // ... session ID extraction works here\n```\n\n**Missing:**\n- No extraction of project field from first-line metadata\n- No extraction of model field from first-line metadata\n- TODO comment at line 229 confirms this is future work\n\n### 5. Required Fields for Goose Plugin\n\nBased on the plan.md (line 60-63) and existing goose.toml:\n\n**Session metadata (line 1):**\n- `working_dir` (string) - Absolute path to project directory\n- `description` (string) - Optional session description\n- Other fields: not specified in docs, need verification\n\n**Message events (subsequent lines):**\n- `role` (string) - \"user\", \"assistant\", \"system\", \"tool\"\n- `content` (array or string) - Message content blocks\n- `timestamp` (string) - ISO 8601 timestamp\n\n**Acceptable values:**\n- `working_dir`: Must be absolute path string (e.g., \"/home/user/project\")\n- `role`: Standard message role enum values\n- `content`: Can be array of content blocks or string\n\n## Implementation Gap\n\n**What works:**\n- ✅ Plugin schema accepts `method = \"field\"` with `field = \"working_dir\"`\n- ✅ First-line reading works for session ID extraction\n- ✅ Envelope unwrapping infrastructure exists\n\n**What doesn't work:**\n- ❌ JSONL parser does NOT extract project from first-line metadata\n- ❌ Current goose.toml config will silently fail (project will be None)\n- ❌ No error message - just falls back to None\n\n## Acceptance Criteria Status\n\n- [x] Schema requirements documented\n- [x] Project detection mechanism understood\n- [ ] **NOT READY** to create config with correct field values - implementation missing\n\n## Next Steps\n\nTo properly support the goose plugin (and other JSONL agents with first-line metadata):\n\n1. Implement project field extraction in jsonl.rs:\n   - Read first line during `detect_sessions()` or `parse()`\n   - Extract field specified in `plugin.parser.project.field`\n   - Store in `SessionInfo.metadata` or directly set on ParseContext\n\n2. Plugin Configuration Schema\n12\t\n13\t### 1.1 Complete Plugin TOML\n14\t\n15\t```toml\n16\t[plugin]\n17\tname = \"goose\"\n18\tversion = \"1.0\"\n19\t\n20\t[source]\n21\tpaths = [\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]\n22\texclude = []\n23\tformat = \"jsonl\"\n24\t\n25\t[source.session_detection]\n26\tmethod = \"one-file-per-session\"\n27\tsession_id_from = \"filename\"\n28\t\n29\t[parser]\n30\ttimestamp = \"created\"        # Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\n31\trole = \"role\"               # \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n32\tcontent = \"content\"         # Array of content blocks\n33\t\n34\t[parser.static]\n35\tsource_agent = \"goose\"\n36\t\n37\t[parser.project]\n38\tmethod = \"field\"            # Extract from first-line metadata\n39\tfield = \"working_dir\"       # Field name in session metadata (line 1)\n40\t\n41\t[parser.model]\n42\tsource = \"none\"             # Model information not available in goose logs\n43\t\n44\t[parser.file_paths]\n45\tcontent_regex = true        # Extract file paths from content via regex\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t### 1.2 Plugin Metadata Fields\n49\t\n50\t| Field | Type | Required | Description |\n51\t|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n52\t| `name` | string | yes | Plugin identifier: \"goose\" |\n53\t| `version` | string | yes | Plugin definition version: \"1.0\" |\n54\t\n55\t---\n56\t\n57\t## 2. Source Configuration\n58\t\n59\t### 2.1 Source Fields\n60\t\n61\t| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n62\t|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n63\t| `paths` | array | yes | glob patterns | `[\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]` |\n64\t| `exclude` | array | no | glob patterns | Patterns to exclude from matches |\n65\t| `format` | string | yes | `\"jsonl\"` | File format identifier |\n66\t\n67\t### 2.2 Session Detection Fields\n68\t\n69\t| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n70\t|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n71\t| `method` | string | yes | `\"one-file-per-session\"` | Each JSONL file is one session |\n72\t| `session_id_from` | string | yes | `\"filename\"` | Session ID extracted from filename |\n73\t\n74\t---\n75\t\n76\t## 3. Parser Field Mapping\n77\t\n78\t### 3.1 Core Parser Fields\n79\t\n80\t| Canonical Field | Source Field | Type | Location | Constraints |\n81\t|----------------|--------------|------|----------|-------------|\n82\t| `timestamp` | `created` | integer | Message lines | Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch) |\n83\t| `role` | `role` | string | Message lines | Must be \"user\" or \"assistant\" |\n84\t| `content` | `content` | array | Message lines | Array of content blocks (see §4) |\n85\t\n86\t### 3.2 Static Fields\n87\t\n88\t| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n89\t|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n90\t| `source_agent` | `\"goose\"` | string | Identifies the agent type in canonical events |\n91\t\n92\t### 3.3 Project Detection Fields\n93\t\n94\t| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n95\t|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n96\t| `method` | `\"field\"` | string | Extract project path from JSON field |\n97\t| `field` | `\"working_dir\"` | string | Field name in first-line session metadata |\n98\t\n99\t**Acceptable values for `working_dir`:**\n100\t- Format: Absolute file path string\n101\t- Example: `\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"`\n102\t- Location: Line 1 of JSONL file (session metadata only)\n103\t\n104\t**Alternative method** (if field-based detection not implemented):\n105\t```toml\n106\t[parser.project]\n107\tmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n108\t```\n109\t\n110\t### 3.4 Model Detection Fields\n111\t\n112\t| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n113\t|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n114\t| `source` | `\"none\"` | string | Model information not available in goose logs |\n115\t\n116\t**Note:** Goose logs do not contain model name information. All sessions will have `model: null` in the canonical schema.\n117\t\n118\t### 3.5 File Path Extraction Fields\n119\t\n120\t| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n121\t|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n122\t| `content_regex` | `true` | boolean | Extract file paths from content using regex patterns |\n123\t\n124\t---\n125\t\n126\t## 4. Data Format Structure\n127\t\n128\t### 4.1 File Structure\n129\t\n130\tGoose session files use a **two-part JSONL structure**:\n131\t\n132\t#### **Line 1: Session Metadata**\n133\t\n134\t```json\n135\t{\n136\t  \"working_dir\": \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\n137\t  \"description\": \"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\n138\t  \"message_count\": 4,\n139\t  \"total_tokens\": 1250,\n140\t  \"input_tokens\": 980,\n141\t  \"output_tokens\": 270,\n142\t  \"accumulated_total_tokens\": 75280,\n143\t  \"accumulated_input_tokens\": 72872,\n144\t  \"accumulated_output_tokens\": 2408\n145\t}\n146\t```\n147\t\n148\t**Session Metadata Fields:**\n149\t\n150\t| Field | Type | Required | Description |\n151\t|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n152\t| `working_dir` | string | yes | **Primary project path** - absolute path to project directory |\n153\t| `description` | string | no | Human-readable session description |\n154\t| `message_count` | integer | yes | Number of messages in this session |\n155\t| `total_tokens` | integer | yes | Token count for this session |\n156\t| `input_tokens` | integer | yes | Input tokens for this session |\n157\t| `output_tokens` | integer | yes | Output tokens for this session |\n158\t| `accumulated_total_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative total tokens across all sessions |\n159\t| `accumulated_input_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative input tokens across all sessions |\n160\t| `accumulated_output_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative output tokens across all sessions |\n161\t\n162\t#### **Subsequent Lines: Message Events**\n163\t\n164\t```json\n165\t{\n166\t  \"role\": \"user\",\n167\t  \"created\": 1747178328,\n168\t  \"content\": [...]\n169\t}\n170\t```\n171\t\n172\t**Message Event Fields:**\n173\t\n174\t| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n175\t|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n176\t| `role` | string | yes | `\"user\"` or `\"assistant\"` | Message role |\n177\t| `created` | integer | yes | Unix timestamp | Seconds since epoch |\n178\t| `content` | array | yes | Content blocks | Array of content block objects (see §4.2) |\n179\t\n180\t### 4.2 Content Block Types\n181\t\n182\tThe `content` array contains blocks of different types that AgentScribe expands into canonical events:\n183\t\n184\t#### Type 1: Text Block\n185\t\n186\t```json\n187\t{\n188\t  \"type\": \"text\",\n189\t  \"text\": \"I have started a basic mcp template...\"\n190\t}\n191\t```\n192\t\n193\t**Fields:**\n194\t- `type`: \"text\"\n195\t- `text`: String content of the message\n196\t\n197\t→ Becomes message content text in canonical event\n198\t\n199\t#### Type 2: Tool Request Block\n200\t\n201\t```json\n202\t{\n203\t  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n204\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n205\t  \"toolCall\": {\n206\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n207\t    \"value\": {\n208\t      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n209\t      \"arguments\": {\n210\t        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n211\t      }\n212\t    }\n213\t  }\n214\t}\n215\t```\n216\t\n217\t**Fields:**\n218\t- `type`: \"toolRequest\"\n219\t- `id`: Tool correlation ID (matches with toolResponse)\n220\t- `toolCall.status`: \"success\" or other status\n221\t- `toolCall.value.name`: Tool name (e.g., \"developer__shell\")\n222\t- `toolCall.value.arguments`: Tool parameters object\n223\t\n224\t→ Expands to `tool_call` canonical event\n225\t\n226\t#### Type 3: Tool Response Block\n227\t\n228\t```json\n229\t{\n230\t  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n231\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n232\t  \"toolResult\": {\n233\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n234\t    \"value\": [...]\n235\t  }\n236\t}\n237\t```\n238\t\n239\t**Fields:**\n240\t- `type`: \"toolResponse\"\n241\t- `id`: Tool correlation ID (matches with toolRequest)\n242\t- `toolResult.status`: \"success\" or other status\n243\t- `toolResult.value`: Tool output (can be nested content blocks)\n244\t\n245\t→ Expands to `tool_result` canonical event\n246\t\n247\t---\n248\t\n249\t## 5. Field Constraints and Validation\n250\t\n251\t### 5.1 Timestamp Field\n252\t\n253\t**Field name:** `created`\n254\t\n255\t**Acceptable values:**\n256\t- Format: Integer (Unix timestamp)\n257\t- Unit: Seconds since epoch\n258\t- Example: `1747178328`\n259\t\n260\t**Constraints:**\n261\t- Must be parseable as integer\n262\t- Represents seconds (not milliseconds)\n263\t- No ISO 8601 format support\n264\t\n265\t### 5.2 Role Field\n266\t\n267\t**Field name:** `role`\n268\t\n269\t**Acceptable values:**\n270\t- `\"user\"` - User message\n271\t- `\"assistant\"` - Assistant message\n272\t\n273\t**Constraints:**\n274\t- Required on all message lines\n275\t- Must be lowercase string\n276\t- No other roles supported (no \"system\", \"tool\", etc. in base role field)\n277\t\n278\t### 5.3 Content Field\n279\t\n280\t**Field name:** `content`\n281\t\n282\t**Acceptable values:**\n283\t- Format: JSON array of content block objects\n284\t- Each block must have `type` field\n285\t- Supported types: \"text\", \"toolRequest\", \"toolResponse\"\n286\t\n287\t**Constraints:**\n288\t- Required on all message lines\n289\t- Must be valid JSON array\n290\t- Cannot be null or undefined\n291\t- Array can contain multiple blocks (e.g., text + toolRequest)\n292\t\n293\t### 5.4 Project Field (working_dir)\n294\t\n295\t**Field name:** `working_dir`\n296\t\n297\t**Acceptable values:**\n298\t- Format: Absolute file path string\n299\t- Example: `\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"`\n300\t\n301\t**Constraints:**\n302\t- Located in line 1 only (session metadata)\n303\t- Must be absolute path (starts with `/`)\n304\t- Should be valid directory path\n305\t- Not present on message lines\n306\t\n307\t---\n308\t\n309\t## 6. Examples and Default Values\n310\t\n311\t### 6.1 Complete Example Session File\n312\t\n313\t```jsonl\n314\t{\"working_dir\":\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\"description\":\"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\"message_count\":4,\"total_tokens\":1250,\"input_tokens\":980,\"output_tokens\":270}\n315\t{\"role\":\"user\",\"created\":1747178328,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"I'm seeing a memory leak in my worker process. Can you help me investigate?\"}]}\n316\t{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"created\":1747178334,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"I'll help you investigate the memory leak. Let's start by checking the current memory usage and looking for potential issues.\"},{\"type\":\"toolRequest\",\"id\":\"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\"toolCall\":{\"status\":\"success\",\"value\":{\"name\":\"developer__shell\",\"arguments\":{\"command\":\"ps aux | grep worker\"}}}}]}\n317\t{\"role\":\"user\",\"created\":1747178334,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"toolResponse\",\"id\":\"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\"toolResult\":{\"status\":\"success\",\"value\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"USER       PID %CPU %MEM    VSZ   RSS TTY      STAT START   TIME COMMAND\\ncoding    1234  2.5  8.5 123456 87654 pts/0    R+   10:30   0:15 node worker.js\\n\"}]}}]}\n318\t```\n319\t\n320\t### 6.2 Default Values\n321\t\n322\t| Field | Default Value | Source |\n323\t|-------|---------------|--------|\n324\t| `source_agent` | `\"goose\"` | Static field in parser config |\n325\t| `model` | `null` | Not available in goose logs |\n326\t| `project` | Extracted from `working_dir` or parent directory | Project detection method |\n327\t| `file_paths` | Extracted via regex from content | `content_regex = true` |\n328\t\n329\t---\n330\t\n331\t## 7. Event Expansion Rules\n332\t\n333\tAgentScribe expands goose content blocks into canonical events as follows:\n334\t\n335\t### 7.1 Text Block → Message Content\n336\t\n337\t```json\n338\t{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hello world\"}\n339\t```\n340\t\n341\t→ Canonical event:\n342\t```json\n343\t{\n344\t  \"role\": \"user\",\n345\t  \"content\": \"Hello world\"\n346\t}\n347\t```\n348\t\n349\t### 7.2 Tool Request → tool_call Event\n350\t\n351\t```json\n352\t{\n353\t  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n354\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n355\t  \"toolCall\": {\n356\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n357\t    \"value\": {\n358\t      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n359\t      \"arguments\": {\n360\t        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n361\t      }\n362\t    }\n363\t  }\n364\t}\n365\t```\n366\t\n367\t→ Canonical event:\n368\t```json\n369\t{\n370\t  \"role\": \"tool_call\",\n371\t  \"tool\": \"developer__shell\",\n372\t  \"content\": \"{\\\"command\\\":\\\"ls -la\\\"}\",\n373\t  \"tool_id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\"\n374\t}\n375\t```\n376\t\n377\t### 7.3 Tool Response → tool_result Event\n378\t\n379\t```json\n380\t{\n381\t  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n382\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n383\t  \"toolResult\": {\n384\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n385\t    \"value\": [...]\n386\t  }\n387\t}\n388\t```\n389\t\n390\t→ Canonical event:\n391\t```json\n392\t{\n393\t  \"role\": \"tool_result\",\n394\t  \"tool\": \"developer__shell\",\n395\t  \"content\": \"...\",\n396\t  \"tool_id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\"\n397\t}\n398\t```\n399\t\n400\t### 7.4 Tool Correlation\n401\t\n402\tTool requests and responses are correlated by the `id` field:\n403\t- `toolRequest.id` == `toolResponse.id` → Same tool interaction\n404\t- ID format: `toolu_<random>_<base62>`\n405\t- AgentScribe matches these to create complete tool call + result pairs\n406\t\n407\t---\n408\t\n409\t## 8. Implementation Notes\n410\t\n411\t### 8.1 Current Implementation Status\n412\t\n413\t**What Works:**\n414\t- ✅ JSONL parsing for standard message lines\n415\t- ✅ Field mapping for timestamp, role, content\n416\t- ✅ Static field assignment (source_agent)\n417\t- ✅ Project detection with `ParentDir` method (fallback)\n418\t\n419\t**Implementation Gaps:**\n420\t- ⚠️ **Field-based project detection for JSONL**: `ProjectDetection::Field` is not fully implemented for JSONL format (parser returns `None` for field-based detection)\n421\t- ⚠️ **First-line metadata extraction**: JSONL parser needs to extract `working_dir` from line 1 and store in ParseContext\n422\t- ⚠️ **content[] array expansion**: Parser needs to expand content blocks into separate canonical events\n423\t\n424\t### 8.2 Current Workaround\n425\t\n426\tIf field-based project detection is not fully implemented, use `ParentDir` method:\n427\t\n428\t```toml\n429\t[parser.project]\n430\tmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n431\t```\n432\t\n433\tThis is less accurate than using `working_dir` from metadata but works immediately.\n434\t\n435\t### 8.3 Differences from Other Agents\n436\t\n437\t| Aspect | Goose | Claude Code | Codex |\n438\t|--------|-------|-------------|-------|\n439\t| **Session metadata location** | Line 1 of JSONL | Separate `session-meta/<uuid>.json` file | Line 1 of JSONL |\n440\t| **Project field** | `working_dir` | `cwd` | `cwd` |\n441\t| **Timestamp format** | Unix timestamp (`created`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) |\n442\t| **Content structure** | `content[]` array with blocks | Embedded in message with `type` field | Nested `content[]` blocks |\n443\t| **Tool calls** | `toolRequest`/`toolResponse` blocks | `tool_use` content blocks | `function_call`/`function_call_output` |\n444\t| **Model tracking** | Not available | In metadata file | In metadata |\n445\t| **Envelope unwrapping** | Not needed | Not needed | Required |\n446\t\n447\t---\n448\t\n449\t## 9. Acceptance Criteria — Status\n450\t\n451\t- ✅ **Schema documentation located and read** — All plugin fields documented\n452\t- ✅ **All required fields listed with types** — Complete field mapping table\n453\t- ✅ **Field constraints documented** — Validation rules for each field\n454\t- ✅ **Examples or defaults noted** — Complete session file example provided\n455\t\n456\t---\n457\t\n458\t## 10. References\n459\t\n460\t- **Plugin source:** `plugins/goose.toml`\n461\t- **Test fixtures:** `tests/fixtures/goose/sample_session.jsonl`\n462\t- **Fixture documentation:** `tests/fixtures/goose/README.md`\n463\t- **Implementation:** `src/parser/jsonl.rs` (JSONL parser)\n464\t- **Schema verification:** https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/issues/2529\n465\t- **Related research:**\n466\t  - `docs/research-goose-plugin-schema.md`\n467\t  - `docs/research/goose-plugin-schema-and-project-detection.md`\n468\t\n469\t---\n470\t\n471\t## Summary\n472\t\n473\tThe goose plugin schema defines a JSONL-based conversation log format with:\n474\t\n475\t1. **First-line session metadata** containing `working_dir` for project detection\n476\t2. **Message events** with `role`, `created` (Unix timestamp), and `content[]` array\n477\t3. **Content block types**: text, toolRequest, toolResponse\n478\t4. **Project detection** via `working_dir` field (line 1) or parent directory fallback\n479\t5. **No model information** available (all sessions have `model: null`)\n480\t6. **Tool correlation** via matching `id` fields between toolRequest and toolResponse blocks\n481\t\n482\tThe plugin configuration maps these native fields to AgentScribe's canonical event schema, enabling unified search and analytics across all supported agent types.\n483\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da9b842c-bed9-4c15-8080-65bf422117f5","uuid":"bb4c429f-5de1-4625-a128-1c7d699ab1ed","timestamp":"2026-08-21T03:27:36.801Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/coding/AgentScribe/docs/research/goose-plugin-schema-complete.md","content":"# Goose Plugin Schema — Complete Documentation\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document provides comprehensive documentation of the goose plugin schema for AgentScribe, including all required fields, types, constraints, and examples based on actual goose log files and plugin configuration.\n\n**Source Schema Verified From:** https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/issues/2529\n\n---\n\n## 1. Plugin Configuration Schema\n\n### 1.1 Complete Plugin TOML\n\n```toml\n[plugin]\nname = \"goose\"\nversion = \"1.0\"\n\n[source]\npaths = [\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]\nexclude = []\nformat = \"jsonl\"\n\n[source.session_detection]\nmethod = \"one-file-per-session\"\nsession_id_from = \"filename\"\n\n[parser]\ntimestamp = \"created\"        # Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\nrole = \"role\"               # \"user\" or \"assistant\"\ncontent = \"content\"         # Array of content blocks\n\n[parser.static]\nsource_agent = \"goose\"\n\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"field\"            # Extract from first-line metadata\nfield = \"working_dir\"       # Field name in session metadata (line 1)\n\n[parser.model]\nsource = \"none\"             # Model information not available in goose logs\n\n[parser.file_paths]\ncontent_regex = true        # Extract file paths from content via regex\n```\n\n### 1.2 Plugin Metadata Fields\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n| `name` | string | yes | Plugin identifier: \"goose\" |\n| `version` | string | yes | Plugin definition version: \"1.0\" |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Source Configuration\n\n### 2.1 Source Fields\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| `paths` | array | yes | glob patterns | `[\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]` |\n| `exclude` | array | no | glob patterns | Patterns to exclude from matches |\n| `format` | string | yes | `\"jsonl\"` | File format identifier |\n\n### 2.2 Session Detection Fields\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| `method` | string | yes | `\"one-file-per-session\"` | Each JSONL file is one session |\n| `session_id_from` | string | yes | `\"filename\"` | Session ID extracted from filename |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Parser Field Mapping\n\n### 3.1 Core Parser Fields\n\n| Canonical Field | Source Field | Type | Location | Constraints |\n|----------------|--------------|------|----------|-------------|\n| `timestamp` | `created` | integer | Message lines | Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch) |\n| `role` | `role` | string | Message lines | Must be \"user\" or \"assistant\" |\n| `content` | `content` | array | Message lines | Array of content blocks (see §4) |\n\n### 3.2 Static Fields\n\n| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n| `source_agent` | `\"goose\"` | string | Identifies the agent type in canonical events |\n\n### 3.3 Project Detection Fields\n\n| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n| `method` | `\"field\"` | string | Extract project path from JSON field |\n| `field` | `\"working_dir\"` | string | Field name in first-line session metadata |\n\n**Acceptable values for `working_dir`:**\n- Format: Absolute file path string\n- Example: `\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"`\n- Location: Line 1 of JSONL file (session metadata only)\n\n**Alternative method** (if field-based detection not implemented):\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n```\n\n### 3.4 Model Detection Fields\n\n| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n| `source` | `\"none\"` | string | Model information not available in goose logs |\n\n**Note:** Goose logs do not contain model name information. All sessions will have `model: null` in the canonical schema.\n\n### 3.5 File Path Extraction Fields\n\n| Field | Value | Type | Description |\n|-------|-------|------|-------------|\n| `content_regex` | `true` | boolean | Extract file paths from content using regex patterns |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Data Format Structure\n\n### 4.1 File Structure\n\nGoose session files use a **two-part JSONL structure**:\n\n#### **Line 1: Session Metadata**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"working_dir\": \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\n  \"description\": \"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\n  \"message_count\": 4,\n  \"total_tokens\": 1250,\n  \"input_tokens\": 980,\n  \"output_tokens\": 270,\n  \"accumulated_total_tokens\": 75280,\n  \"accumulated_input_tokens\": 72872,\n  \"accumulated_output_tokens\": 2408\n}\n```\n\n**Session Metadata Fields:**\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n| `working_dir` | string | yes | **Primary project path** - absolute path to project directory |\n| `description` | string | no | Human-readable session description |\n| `message_count` | integer | yes | Number of messages in this session |\n| `total_tokens` | integer | yes | Token count for this session |\n| `input_tokens` | integer | yes | Input tokens for this session |\n| `output_tokens` | integer | yes | Output tokens for this session |\n| `accumulated_total_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative total tokens across all sessions |\n| `accumulated_input_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative input tokens across all sessions |\n| `accumulated_output_tokens` | integer | yes | Cumulative output tokens across all sessions |\n\n#### **Subsequent Lines: Message Events**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"role\": \"user\",\n  \"created\": 1747178328,\n  \"content\": [...]\n}\n```\n\n**Message Event Fields:**\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Values | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| `role` | string | yes | `\"user\"` or `\"assistant\"` | Message role |\n| `created` | integer | yes | Unix timestamp | Seconds since epoch |\n| `content` | array | yes | Content blocks | Array of content block objects (see §4.2) |\n\n### 4.2 Content Block Types\n\nThe `content` array contains blocks of different types that AgentScribe expands into canonical events:\n\n#### Type 1: Text Block\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"text\",\n  \"text\": \"I have started a basic mcp template...\"\n}\n```\n\n**Fields:**\n- `type`: \"text\"\n- `text`: String content of the message\n\n→ Becomes message content text in canonical event\n\n#### Type 2: Tool Request Block\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolCall\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": {\n      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Fields:**\n- `type`: \"toolRequest\"\n- `id`: Tool correlation ID (matches with toolResponse)\n- `toolCall.status`: \"success\" or other status\n- `toolCall.value.name`: Tool name (e.g., \"developer__shell\")\n- `toolCall.value.arguments`: Tool parameters object\n\n→ Expands to `tool_call` canonical event\n\n#### Type 3: Tool Response Block\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolResult\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": [...]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Fields:**\n- `type`: \"toolResponse\"\n- `id`: Tool correlation ID (matches with toolRequest)\n- `toolResult.status`: \"success\" or other status\n- `toolResult.value`: Tool output (can be nested content blocks)\n\n→ Expands to `tool_result` canonical event\n\n---\n\n## 5. Field Constraints and Validation\n\n### 5.1 Timestamp Field\n\n**Field name:** `created`\n\n**Acceptable values:**\n- Format: Integer (Unix timestamp)\n- Unit: Seconds since epoch\n- Example: `1747178328`\n\n**Constraints:**\n- Must be parseable as integer\n- Represents seconds (not milliseconds)\n- No ISO 8601 format support\n\n### 5.2 Role Field\n\n**Field name:** `role`\n\n**Acceptable values:**\n- `\"user\"` - User message\n- `\"assistant\"` - Assistant message\n\n**Constraints:**\n- Required on all message lines\n- Must be lowercase string\n- No other roles supported (no \"system\", \"tool\", etc. in base role field)\n\n### 5.3 Content Field\n\n**Field name:** `content`\n\n**Acceptable values:**\n- Format: JSON array of content block objects\n- Each block must have `type` field\n- Supported types: \"text\", \"toolRequest\", \"toolResponse\"\n\n**Constraints:**\n- Required on all message lines\n- Must be valid JSON array\n- Cannot be null or undefined\n- Array can contain multiple blocks (e.g., text + toolRequest)\n\n### 5.4 Project Field (working_dir)\n\n**Field name:** `working_dir`\n\n**Acceptable values:**\n- Format: Absolute file path string\n- Example: `\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"`\n\n**Constraints:**\n- Located in line 1 only (session metadata)\n- Must be absolute path (starts with `/`)\n- Should be valid directory path\n- Not present on message lines\n\n---\n\n## 6. Examples and Default Values\n\n### 6.1 Complete Example Session File\n\n```jsonl\n{\"working_dir\":\"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\"description\":\"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\"message_count\":4,\"total_tokens\":1250,\"input_tokens\":980,\"output_tokens\":270}\n{\"role\":\"user\",\"created\":1747178328,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"I'm seeing a memory leak in my worker process. Can you help me investigate?\"}]}\n{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"created\":1747178334,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"I'll help you investigate the memory leak. Let's start by checking the current memory usage and looking for potential issues.\"},{\"type\":\"toolRequest\",\"id\":\"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\"toolCall\":{\"status\":\"success\",\"value\":{\"name\":\"developer__shell\",\"arguments\":{\"command\":\"ps aux | grep worker\"}}}}]}\n{\"role\":\"user\",\"created\":1747178334,\"content\":[{\"type\":\"toolResponse\",\"id\":\"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\"toolResult\":{\"status\":\"success\",\"value\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"USER       PID %CPU %MEM    VSZ   RSS TTY      STAT START   TIME COMMAND\\ncoding    1234  2.5  8.5 123456 87654 pts/0    R+   10:30   0:15 node worker.js\\n\"}]}}]}\n```\n\n### 6.2 Default Values\n\n| Field | Default Value | Source |\n|-------|---------------|--------|\n| `source_agent` | `\"goose\"` | Static field in parser config |\n| `model` | `null` | Not available in goose logs |\n| `project` | Extracted from `working_dir` or parent directory | Project detection method |\n| `file_paths` | Extracted via regex from content | `content_regex = true` |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Event Expansion Rules\n\nAgentScribe expands goose content blocks into canonical events as follows:\n\n### 7.1 Text Block → Message Content\n\n```json\n{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hello world\"}\n```\n\n→ Canonical event:\n```json\n{\n  \"role\": \"user\",\n  \"content\": \"Hello world\"\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 Tool Request → tool_call Event\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolCall\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": {\n      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n→ Canonical event:\n```json\n{\n  \"role\": \"tool_call\",\n  \"tool\": \"developer__shell\",\n  \"content\": \"{\\\"command\\\":\\\"ls -la\\\"}\",\n  \"tool_id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\"\n}\n```\n\n### 7.3 Tool Response → tool_result Event\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolResult\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": [...]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n→ Canonical event:\n```json\n{\n  \"role\": \"tool_result\",\n  \"tool\": \"developer__shell\",\n  \"content\": \"...\",\n  \"tool_id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\"\n}\n```\n\n### 7.4 Tool Correlation\n\nTool requests and responses are correlated by the `id` field:\n- `toolRequest.id` == `toolResponse.id` → Same tool interaction\n- ID format: `toolu_<random>_<base62>`\n- AgentScribe matches these to create complete tool call + result pairs\n\n---\n\n## 8. Implementation Notes\n\n### 8.1 Current Implementation Status\n\n**What Works:**\n- ✅ JSONL parsing for standard message lines\n- ✅ Field mapping for timestamp, role, content\n- ✅ Static field assignment (source_agent)\n- ✅ Project detection with `ParentDir` method (fallback)\n\n**Implementation Gaps:**\n- ⚠️ **Field-based project detection for JSONL**: `ProjectDetection::Field` is not fully implemented for JSONL format (parser returns `None` for field-based detection)\n- ⚠️ **First-line metadata extraction**: JSONL parser needs to extract `working_dir` from line 1 and store in ParseContext\n- ⚠️ **content[] array expansion**: Parser needs to expand content blocks into separate canonical events\n\n### 8.2 Current Workaround\n\nIf field-based project detection is not fully implemented, use `ParentDir` method:\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n```\n\nThis is less accurate than using `working_dir` from metadata but works immediately.\n\n### 8.3 Differences from Other Agents\n\n| Aspect | Goose | Claude Code | Codex |\n|--------|-------|-------------|-------|\n| **Session metadata location** | Line 1 of JSONL | Separate `session-meta/<uuid>.json` file | Line 1 of JSONL |\n| **Project field** | `working_dir` | `cwd` | `cwd` |\n| **Timestamp format** | Unix timestamp (`created`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) |\n| **Content structure** | `content[]` array with blocks | Embedded in message with `type` field | Nested `content[]` blocks |\n| **Tool calls** | `toolRequest`/`toolResponse` blocks | `tool_use` content blocks | `function_call`/`function_call_output` |\n| **Model tracking** | Not available | In metadata file | In metadata |\n| **Envelope unwrapping** | Not needed | Not needed | Required |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Acceptance Criteria — Status\n\n- ✅ **Schema documentation located and read** — All plugin fields documented\n- ✅ **All required fields listed with types** — Complete field mapping table\n- ✅ **Field constraints documented** — Validation rules for each field\n- ✅ **Examples or defaults noted** — Complete session file example provided\n\n---\n\n## 10. References\n\n- **Plugin source:** `plugins/goose.toml`\n- **Test fixtures:** `tests/fixtures/goose/sample_session.jsonl`\n- **Fixture documentation:** `tests/fixtures/goose/README.md`\n- **Implementation:** `src/parser/jsonl.rs` (JSONL parser)\n- **Schema verification:** https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/issues/2529\n- **Related research:**\n  - `docs/research-goose-plugin-schema.md`\n  - `docs/research/goose-plugin-schema-and-project-detection.md`\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\nThe goose plugin schema defines a JSONL-based conversation log format with:\n\n1. **First-line session metadata** containing `working_dir` for project detection\n2. **Message events** with `role`, `created` (Unix timestamp), and `content[]` array\n3. **Content block types**: text, toolRequest, toolResponse\n4. **Project detection** via `working_dir` field (line 1) or parent directory fallback\n5. **No model information** available (all sessions have `model: null`)\n6. **Tool correlation** via matching `id` fields between toolRequest and toolResponse blocks\n\nThe plugin configuration maps these native fields to AgentScribe's canonical event schema, enabling unified search and analytics across all supported agent types.\n","numLines":483,"startLine":1,"totalLines":483}}}

Goose Plugin Schema\n8\t\n9\t### 1.1 Data Location\n10\t\n11\t- **Path**: `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl`\n12\t- **Format**: JSONL (one JSON object per line)\n13\t\n14\t### 1.2 File Structure\n15\t\n16\tGoose session files have a specific two-part structure:\n17\t\n18\t#### **Line 1: Session Metadata**\n19\t\n20\t```json\n21\t{\n22\t  \"working_dir\": \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\n23\t  \"description\": \"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\n24\t  \"message_count\": 4,\n25\t  \"total_tokens\": 1250,\n26\t  \"input_tokens\": 980,\n27\t  \"output_tokens\": 270,\n28\t  \"accumulated_total_tokens\": 75280,\n29\t  \"accumulated_input_tokens\": 72872,\n30\t  \"accumulated_output_tokens\": 2408\n31\t}\n32\t```\n33\t\n34\t**Key fields:**\n35\t- `working_dir`: **Primary project path field** - absolute path to the project directory\n36\t- `description`: Human-readable session description\n37\t- `message_count`: Number of messages in the session\n38\t- `total_tokens`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`: Token counts for this session\n39\t- `accumulated_*`: Cumulative token counts across sessions\n40\t\n41\t#### **Subsequent Lines: Messages**\n42\t\n43\t```json\n44\t{\n45\t  \"role\": \"user\",\n46\t  \"created\": 1747178328,\n47\t  \"content\": [\n48\t    {\n49\t      \"type\": \"text\",\n50\t      \"text\": \"I'm seeing a memory leak in my worker process. Can you help me investigate?\"\n51\t    }\n52\t  ]\n53\t}\n54\t```\n55\t\n56\t**Message fields:**\n57\t- `role`: \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n58\t- `created`: Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\n59\t- `content`: Array of content blocks (text, toolRequest, toolResponse)\n60\t\n61\t### 1.3 Content Block Types\n62\t\n63\tThe `content` array contains blocks of different types that AgentScribe expands into canonical events:\n64\t\n65\t#### Text Block\n66\t```json\n67\t{\n68\t  \"type\": \"text\",\n69\t  \"text\": \"I have started a basic mcp template...\"\n70\t}\n71\t```\n72\t→ Becomes message content text\n73\t\n74\t#### Tool Request Block\n75\t```json\n76\t{\n77\t  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n78\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n79\t  \"toolCall\": {\n80\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n81\t    \"value\": {\n82\t      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n83\t      \"arguments\": {\n84\t        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n85\t      }\n86\t    }\n87\t  }\n88\t}\n89\t```\n90\t→ Expands to `tool_call` event (extracts tool name from `toolCall.value.name`)\n91\t\n92\t#### Tool Response Block\n93\t```json\n94\t{\n95\t  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n96\t  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n97\t  \"toolResult\": {\n98\t    \"status\": \"success\",\n99\t    \"value\": [...]\n100\t  }\n101\t}\n102\t```\n103\t→ Expands to `tool_result` event (correlated with tool_call by `id`)\n104\t\n105\t### 1.4 Required Fields for Plugin Configuration\n106\t\n107\tFor goose, the essential field mappings are:\n108\t\n109\t| Canonical Field | Source Field | Notes |\n110\t|----------------|--------------|-------|\n111\t| `timestamp` | `created` | Unix timestamp (seconds) |\n112\t| `role` | `role` | \"user\" or \"assistant\" |\n113\t| `content` | `content` | Array of content blocks |\n114\t| `project` | `working_dir` | From first-line metadata (line 1) |\n115\t\n116\t## 2. Project Detection Mechanisms\n117\t\n118\t### 2.1 ProjectDetection Enum\n119\t\n120\tAgentScribe supports three project detection strategies defined in `src/plugin.rs`:\n121\t\n122\t```rust\n123\tpub enum ProjectDetection {\n124\t    Field { field: String },  // Extract from a JSON field\n125\t    ParentDir,                // Use parent directory of log file\n126\t    GitRoot,                  // Use git repository root\n127\t}\n128\t```\n129\t\n130\t### 2.2 How ProjectDetection::Field Works\n131\t\n132\t#### Configuration (in `plugins/goose.toml`)\n133\t\n134\t```toml\n135\t[parser.project]\n136\tmethod = \"field\"\n137\tfield = \"working_dir\"\n138\t```\n139\t\n140\t#### Implementation (from `src/scraper/mod.rs`)\n141\t\n142\tThe scraper's `detect_project()` method handles field-based detection:\n143\t\n144\t```rust\n145\t// Line 663-667 in src/scraper/mod.rs\n146\tProjectDetection::Field { field: _ } => {\n147\t    // For field-based detection, we need to extract from the first event\n148\t    // This is handled in the parser, return None here\n149\t    Ok(None)\n150\t}\n151\t```\n152\t\n153\tThis means field-based project detection is **parser-specific**, not scraper-level.\n154\t\n155\t#### Parser Implementation\n156\t\n157\tDifferent parsers handle field-based project detection differently:\n158\t\n159\t**For JSON Array format** (`src/parser/json_array.rs` lines 175-181):\n160\t\n161\t```rust\n162\t// Set project: prefer field extraction from event, fall back to context\n163\tevent.project =\n164\t    if let Some(ProjectDetection::Field { field }) = plugin.parser.project.as_ref() {\n165\t        extract_string(item, field).or_else(|| context.project.clone())\n166\t    } else {\n167\t        context.project.clone()\n168\t    };\n169\t```\n170\t\n171\tThis extracts the project from **each event item**.\n172\t\n173\t**For JSONL format** (`src/parser/jsonl.rs` line 387):\n174\t\n175\t```rust\n176\t// Set project from context\n177\tevent.project = context.project.clone();\n178\t```\n179\t\n180\tJSONL uses the **ParseContext** to carry project information across all events.\n181\t\n182\t### 2.3 How First-Line Metadata Sets Project\n183\t\n184\tThe key insight is that the first line of goose JSONL files contains the session metadata with the `working_dir` field. Here's how it flows:\n185\t\n186\t1. **Parser reads line 1**: Session metadata with `working_dir`\n187\t2. **Extract project field**: Parser should extract `working_dir` from line 1\n188\t3. **Store in ParseContext**: Project value is stored in `ParseContext.project`\n189\t4. **Apply to all events**: Every subsequent event gets `event.project = context.project.clone()`\n190\t\n191\t#### Current Implementation Gap\n192\t\n193\tLooking at the existing code, there's a **missing implementation** for extracting project from first-line metadata in JSONL format:\n194\t\n195\t- The scraper calls `detect_project()` which returns `Ok(None)` for Field detection\n196\t- The ParseContext is created with `project: None` (line 414 in `src/parser/mod.rs`)\n197\t- Events get `event.project = context.project.clone()` which is `None`\n198\t\n199\t**The missing piece**: The JSONL parser needs to:\n200\t1. Read the first line\n201\t2. Extract the project field (e.g., `working_dir`)\n202\t3. Create ParseContext with that project value\n203\t4. Use that context for parsing all subsequent lines\n204\t\n205\t### 2.4 Alternative: ParentDir Detection\n206\t\n207\tIf field-based detection is not implemented, goose can use `ParentDir`:\n208\t\n209\t```toml\n210\t[parser.project]\n211\tmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n212\t```\n213\t\n214\tThis is simpler but less accurate than using the actual `working_dir` from the session metadata.\n215\t\n216\t## 3. Envelope System for First-Line Metadata\n217\t\n218\t### 3.1 What is the Envelope System?\n219\t\n220\tAgentScribe has an **envelope unwrapping system** for handling wrapped JSONL lines where event data is nested inside a wrapper structure (like Codex's `{type, timestamp, payload}` format).\n221\t\n222\t### 3.2 Envelope Configuration\n223\t\n224\t```toml\n225\t[source.envelope]\n226\tpayload_field = \"payload\"    # Field containing actual event data\n227\ttype_field = \"type\"          # Field for routing decisions\n228\ttype_routing = {             # Maps type values to actions\n229\t    message = \"event\",       # → produce canonical events\n230\t    session = \"skip\",        # → ignore this line\n231\t    compaction = \"meta\"      # → accumulate metadata (future)\n232\t}\n233\t```\n234\t\n235\t### 3.3 Routing Actions\n236\t\n237\t- **`\"event\"`**: Extract payload, produce canonical events\n238\t- **`\"skip\"`**: Ignore this line entirely\n239\t- **`\"meta\"`**: Accumulate metadata (not yet fully implemented - returns `Vec::new()`)\n240\t\n241\t### 3.4 Does Goose Need Envelope Unwrapping?\n242\t\n243\t**Current goose plugin**: No envelope configured\n244\t\n245\t**Schema analysis**: Looking at the goose format:\n246\t- Line 1: `{working_dir, description, ...}` - no `type` field, no wrapper\n247\t- Message lines: `{role, created, content[]}` - no wrapper\n248\t\n249\t**Conclusion**: Goose does **NOT** need envelope unwrapping. It's a flat JSONL structure.\n250\t\n251\tThe first line is just a different JSON schema than subsequent lines, not a wrapped structure.\n252\t\n253\t### 3.5 How First-Line Metadata Should Work for Goose\n254\t\n255\tSince goose doesn't use envelope unwrapping, the project extraction should work as follows:\n256\t\n257\t1. **JSONL parser reads first line**: Parse as JSON\n258\t2. **Extract `working_dir` field**: Use the `ProjectDetection::Field { field: \"working_dir\" }` config\n259\t3. **Create ParseContext with project**: \n260\t   ```rust\n261\t   let project = extract_string(&first_line_json, \"working_dir\");\n262\t   let context = ParseContext::new(session_id, source_agent, source_file)\n263\t       .with_project(project);\n264\t   ```\n265\t4. **Parse rest of file with context**: All events get the project from context\n266\t\n267\t## 4. Required Plugin Configuration Fields\n268\t\n269\t### 4.1 Essential Fields for Goose Plugin\n270\t\n271\t```toml\n272\t[plugin]\n273\tname = \"goose\"\n274\tversion = \"1.0\"\n275\t\n276\t[source]\n277\tpaths = [\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]\n278\tformat = \"jsonl\"\n279\t\n280\t[source.session_detection]\n281\tmethod = \"one-file-per-session\"\n282\tsession_id_from = \"filename\"\n283\t\n284\t[parser]\n285\ttimestamp = \"created\"        # Unix timestamp\n286\trole = \"role\"               # \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n287\tcontent = \"content\"         # Array of content blocks\n288\t\n289\t[parser.static]\n290\tsource_agent = \"goose\"\n291\t\n292\t[parser.project]\n293\tmethod = \"field\"            # Extract from first-line metadata\n294\tfield = \"working_dir\"       # Field name in line 1\n295\t\n296\t[parser.model]\n297\tsource = \"none\"             # Model not available in goose logs\n298\t\n299\t[parser.file_paths]\n300\tcontent_regex = true        # Extract paths from content\n301\t```\n302\t\n303\t### 4.2 Field Value Acceptance\n304\t\n305\t#### timestamp field\n306\t- **Accepts**: Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\n307\t- **Format**: Integer or string representing seconds\n308\t- **Example**: `1747178328`\n309\t\n310\t#### role field\n311\t- **Accepts**: \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n312\t- **Format**: String\n313\t- **Required**: Yes - every message line must have a role\n314\t\n315\t#### content field\n316\t- **Accepts**: Array of content blocks\n317\t- **Format**: JSON array with objects containing `type` field\n318\t- **Block types**: \"text\", \"toolRequest\", \"toolResponse\"\n319\t\n320\t#### project field (working_dir)\n321\t- **Accepts**: Absolute file path\n322\t- **Format**: String path\n323\t- **Example**: \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"\n324\t- **Location**: Line 1 only (session metadata)\n325\t\n326\t## 5. Comparison with Other Plugins\n327\t\n328\t### 5.1 vs Claude Code\n329\t\n330\t| Aspect | Goose | Claude Code |\n331\t|--------|-------|-------------|\n332\t| **Session metadata** | Line 1 of JSONL | Separate `session-meta/<uuid>.json` file |\n333\t| **Project field** | `working_dir` | `cwd` |\n334\t| **Timestamp format** | Unix timestamp (`created`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) |\n335\t| **Content structure** | `content[]` array with blocks | Embedded in message with `type` field |\n336\t| **Tool calls** | `toolRequest`/`toolResponse` blocks | `tool_use` content blocks |\n337\t| **Model tracking** | Not available | In metadata file |\n338\t\n339\t### 5.2 vs Codex\n340\t\n341\t| Aspect | Goose | Codex |\n342\t|--------|-------|-------|\n343\t| **Structure** | Flat JSONL | Envelope-wrapped JSONL |\n344\t| **First line** | Session metadata | `RolloutLine::Meta` |\n345\t| **Type routing** | Not needed | Required (skip/meta/event) |\n346\t| **Project field** | `working_dir` (line 1) | `cwd` (in meta) |\n347\t\n348\t## 6. Current Implementation Status\n349\t\n350\t### 6.1 What Works\n351\t\n352\t- ✅ JSONL parsing for standard message lines\n353\t- ✅ Field mapping for timestamp, role, content\n354\t- ✅ Static field assignment (source_agent)\n355\t- ✅ Project detection with ParentDir method\n356\t\n357\t### 6.2 What Needs Implementation\n358\t\n359\t- ⚠️ **First-line metadata extraction**: JSONL parser needs to read line 1, extract project field, and store in ParseContext\n360\t- ⚠️ **Field-based project detection for JSONL**: Currently only implemented for JsonArray format, not Jsonl\n361\t- ⚠️ **content[] array expansion**: Parser needs to expand content blocks into separate events (text → content, toolRequest → tool_call, toolResponse → tool_result)\n362\t\n363\t### 6.3 Current Workaround\n364\t\n365\tThe current goose plugin configuration uses:\n366\t\n367\t```toml\n368\t[parser.project]\n369\tmethod = \"field\"\n370\tfield = \"working_dir\"\n371\t```\n372\t\n373\tBut this relies on parser-level extraction that may not be fully implemented. A working alternative is:\n374\t\n375\t```toml\n376\t[parser.project]\n377\tmethod = \"parent_dir\"\n378\t```\n379\t\n380\tThis uses the parent directory of the session file as the project path, which is less accurate but functional.\n381\t\n382\t## 7. Summary and Recommendations\n383\t\n384\t### 7.1 Schema Requirements\n385\t\n386\tThe goose plugin requires:\n387\t1. **Line 1 handling**: Extract session metadata (`working_dir`, `description`, etc.)\n388\t2. **Field-based project detection**: Implement `ProjectDetection::Field` for JSONL format\n389\t3. **Content array expansion**: Parse `content[]` blocks into canonical events\n390\t4. **Tool correlation**: Match `toolRequest.id` with `toolResponse.id`\n391\t\n392\t### 7.2 Project Detection Field Values\n393\t\n394\tFor goose, the project detection field is:\n395\t- **Field name**: `working_dir`\n396\t- **Location**: Line 1 (first line of JSONL file)\n397\t- **Format**: Absolute file path string\n398\t- **Example value**: `/home/coding/projects/my-app`\n399\t\n400\t### 7.3 Ready to Create Config\n401\t\n402\tWith this understanding, you can create a functional goose plugin configuration using:\n403\t\n404\t1. **Project detection**: `method = \"field\"` with `field = \"working_dir\"` (requires parser implementation) OR `method = \"parent_dir\"` (works immediately)\n405\t2. **Timestamp field**: `timestamp = \"created\"` (Unix timestamp)\n406\t3. **Role field**: `role = \"role\"` (user/assistant)\n407\t4. **Content field**: `content = \"content\"` (array of blocks)\n408\t\n409\t### 7.4 Next Steps\n410\t\n411\tTo fully support goose with accurate project detection:\n412\t\n413\t1. Implement first-line metadata extraction in JSONL parser\n414\t2. Implement content[] array expansion\n415\t3. Add tool request/response correlation\n416\t4. Goose Plugin Schema\n\n### 1.1 Data Location\n\n- **Path**: `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl`\n- **Format**: JSONL (one JSON object per line)\n\n### 1.2 File Structure\n\nGoose session files have a specific two-part structure:\n\n#### **Line 1: Session Metadata**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"working_dir\": \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\",\n  \"description\": \"Debug memory leak in worker process\",\n  \"message_count\": 4,\n  \"total_tokens\": 1250,\n  \"input_tokens\": 980,\n  \"output_tokens\": 270,\n  \"accumulated_total_tokens\": 75280,\n  \"accumulated_input_tokens\": 72872,\n  \"accumulated_output_tokens\": 2408\n}\n```\n\n**Key fields:**\n- `working_dir`: **Primary project path field** - absolute path to the project directory\n- `description`: Human-readable session description\n- `message_count`: Number of messages in the session\n- `total_tokens`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`: Token counts for this session\n- `accumulated_*`: Cumulative token counts across sessions\n\n#### **Subsequent Lines: Messages**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"role\": \"user\",\n  \"created\": 1747178328,\n  \"content\": [\n    {\n      \"type\": \"text\",\n      \"text\": \"I'm seeing a memory leak in my worker process. Can you help me investigate?\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n**Message fields:**\n- `role`: \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n- `created`: Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\n- `content`: Array of content blocks (text, toolRequest, toolResponse)\n\n### 1.3 Content Block Types\n\nThe `content` array contains blocks of different types that AgentScribe expands into canonical events:\n\n#### Text Block\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"text\",\n  \"text\": \"I have started a basic mcp template...\"\n}\n```\n→ Becomes message content text\n\n#### Tool Request Block\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolRequest\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolCall\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": {\n      \"name\": \"developer__shell\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"command\": \"ls -la\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n→ Expands to `tool_call` event (extracts tool name from `toolCall.value.name`)\n\n#### Tool Response Block\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"toolResponse\",\n  \"id\": \"toolu_bdrk_01H6Vzjip2dzWDWJMQkiwevZ\",\n  \"toolResult\": {\n    \"status\": \"success\",\n    \"value\": [...]\n  }\n}\n```\n→ Expands to `tool_result` event (correlated with tool_call by `id`)\n\n### 1.4 Required Fields for Plugin Configuration\n\nFor goose, the essential field mappings are:\n\n| Canonical Field | Source Field | Notes |\n|----------------|--------------|-------|\n| `timestamp` | `created` | Unix timestamp (seconds) |\n| `role` | `role` | \"user\" or \"assistant\" |\n| `content` | `content` | Array of content blocks |\n| `project` | `working_dir` | From first-line metadata (line 1) |\n\n## 2. Project Detection Mechanisms\n\n### 2.1 ProjectDetection Enum\n\nAgentScribe supports three project detection strategies defined in `src/plugin.rs`:\n\n```rust\npub enum ProjectDetection {\n    Field { field: String },  // Extract from a JSON field\n    ParentDir,                // Use parent directory of log file\n    GitRoot,                  // Use git repository root\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 How ProjectDetection::Field Works\n\n#### Configuration (in `plugins/goose.toml`)\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"field\"\nfield = \"working_dir\"\n```\n\n#### Implementation (from `src/scraper/mod.rs`)\n\nThe scraper's `detect_project()` method handles field-based detection:\n\n```rust\n// Line 663-667 in src/scraper/mod.rs\nProjectDetection::Field { field: _ } => {\n    // For field-based detection, we need to extract from the first event\n    // This is handled in the parser, return None here\n    Ok(None)\n}\n```\n\nThis means field-based project detection is **parser-specific**, not scraper-level.\n\n#### Parser Implementation\n\nDifferent parsers handle field-based project detection differently:\n\n**For JSON Array format** (`src/parser/json_array.rs` lines 175-181):\n\n```rust\n// Set project: prefer field extraction from event, fall back to context\nevent.project =\n    if let Some(ProjectDetection::Field { field }) = plugin.parser.project.as_ref() {\n        extract_string(item, field).or_else(|| context.project.clone())\n    } else {\n        context.project.clone()\n    };\n```\n\nThis extracts the project from **each event item**.\n\n**For JSONL format** (`src/parser/jsonl.rs` line 387):\n\n```rust\n// Set project from context\nevent.project = context.project.clone();\n```\n\nJSONL uses the **ParseContext** to carry project information across all events.\n\n### 2.3 How First-Line Metadata Sets Project\n\nThe key insight is that the first line of goose JSONL files contains the session metadata with the `working_dir` field. Here's how it flows:\n\n1. **Parser reads line 1**: Session metadata with `working_dir`\n2. **Extract project field**: Parser should extract `working_dir` from line 1\n3. **Store in ParseContext**: Project value is stored in `ParseContext.project`\n4. **Apply to all events**: Every subsequent event gets `event.project = context.project.clone()`\n\n#### Current Implementation Gap\n\nLooking at the existing code, there's a **missing implementation** for extracting project from first-line metadata in JSONL format:\n\n- The scraper calls `detect_project()` which returns `Ok(None)` for Field detection\n- The ParseContext is created with `project: None` (line 414 in `src/parser/mod.rs`)\n- Events get `event.project = context.project.clone()` which is `None`\n\n**The missing piece**: The JSONL parser needs to:\n1. Read the first line\n2. Extract the project field (e.g., `working_dir`)\n3. Create ParseContext with that project value\n4. Use that context for parsing all subsequent lines\n\n### 2.4 Alternative: ParentDir Detection\n\nIf field-based detection is not implemented, goose can use `ParentDir`:\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"parent_dir\"  # Uses parent directory of session file\n```\n\nThis is simpler but less accurate than using the actual `working_dir` from the session metadata.\n\n## 3. Envelope System for First-Line Metadata\n\n### 3.1 What is the Envelope System?\n\nAgentScribe has an **envelope unwrapping system** for handling wrapped JSONL lines where event data is nested inside a wrapper structure (like Codex's `{type, timestamp, payload}` format).\n\n### 3.2 Envelope Configuration\n\n```toml\n[source.envelope]\npayload_field = \"payload\"    # Field containing actual event data\ntype_field = \"type\"          # Field for routing decisions\ntype_routing = {             # Maps type values to actions\n    message = \"event\",       # → produce canonical events\n    session = \"skip\",        # → ignore this line\n    compaction = \"meta\"      # → accumulate metadata (future)\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Routing Actions\n\n- **`\"event\"`**: Extract payload, produce canonical events\n- **`\"skip\"`**: Ignore this line entirely\n- **`\"meta\"`**: Accumulate metadata (not yet fully implemented - returns `Vec::new()`)\n\n### 3.4 Does Goose Need Envelope Unwrapping?\n\n**Current goose plugin**: No envelope configured\n\n**Schema analysis**: Looking at the goose format:\n- Line 1: `{working_dir, description, ...}` - no `type` field, no wrapper\n- Message lines: `{role, created, content[]}` - no wrapper\n\n**Conclusion**: Goose does **NOT** need envelope unwrapping. It's a flat JSONL structure.\n\nThe first line is just a different JSON schema than subsequent lines, not a wrapped structure.\n\n### 3.5 How First-Line Metadata Should Work for Goose\n\nSince goose doesn't use envelope unwrapping, the project extraction should work as follows:\n\n1. **JSONL parser reads first line**: Parse as JSON\n2. **Extract `working_dir` field**: Use the `ProjectDetection::Field { field: \"working_dir\" }` config\n3. **Create ParseContext with project**: \n   ```rust\n   let project = extract_string(&first_line_json, \"working_dir\");\n   let context = ParseContext::new(session_id, source_agent, source_file)\n       .with_project(project);\n   ```\n4. **Parse rest of file with context**: All events get the project from context\n\n## 4. Required Plugin Configuration Fields\n\n### 4.1 Essential Fields for Goose Plugin\n\n```toml\n[plugin]\nname = \"goose\"\nversion = \"1.0\"\n\n[source]\npaths = [\"~/.local/share/goose/sessions/*.jsonl\"]\nformat = \"jsonl\"\n\n[source.session_detection]\nmethod = \"one-file-per-session\"\nsession_id_from = \"filename\"\n\n[parser]\ntimestamp = \"created\"        # Unix timestamp\nrole = \"role\"               # \"user\" or \"assistant\"\ncontent = \"content\"         # Array of content blocks\n\n[parser.static]\nsource_agent = \"goose\"\n\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"field\"            # Extract from first-line metadata\nfield = \"working_dir\"       # Field name in line 1\n\n[parser.model]\nsource = \"none\"             # Model not available in goose logs\n\n[parser.file_paths]\ncontent_regex = true        # Extract paths from content\n```\n\n### 4.2 Field Value Acceptance\n\n#### timestamp field\n- **Accepts**: Unix timestamp (seconds since epoch)\n- **Format**: Integer or string representing seconds\n- **Example**: `1747178328`\n\n#### role field\n- **Accepts**: \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n- **Format**: String\n- **Required**: Yes - every message line must have a role\n\n#### content field\n- **Accepts**: Array of content blocks\n- **Format**: JSON array with objects containing `type` field\n- **Block types**: \"text\", \"toolRequest\", \"toolResponse\"\n\n#### project field (working_dir)\n- **Accepts**: Absolute file path\n- **Format**: String path\n- **Example**: \"/home/coding/projects/my-app\"\n- **Location**: Line 1 only (session metadata)\n\n## 5. Comparison with Other Plugins\n\n### 5.1 vs Claude Code\n\n| Aspect | Goose | Claude Code |\n|--------|-------|-------------|\n| **Session metadata** | Line 1 of JSONL | Separate `session-meta/<uuid>.json` file |\n| **Project field** | `working_dir` | `cwd` |\n| **Timestamp format** | Unix timestamp (`created`) | ISO 8601 (`timestamp`) |\n| **Content structure** | `content[]` array with blocks | Embedded in message with `type` field |\n| **Tool calls** | `toolRequest`/`toolResponse` blocks | `tool_use` content blocks |\n| **Model tracking** | Not available | In metadata file |\n\n### 5.2 vs Codex\n\n| Aspect | Goose | Codex |\n|--------|-------|-------|\n| **Structure** | Flat JSONL | Envelope-wrapped JSONL |\n| **First line** | Session metadata | `RolloutLine::Meta` |\n| **Type routing** | Not needed | Required (skip/meta/event) |\n| **Project field** | `working_dir` (line 1) | `cwd` (in meta) |\n\n## 6. Current Implementation Status\n\n### 6.1 What Works\n\n- ✅ JSONL parsing for standard message lines\n- ✅ Field mapping for timestamp, role, content\n- ✅ Static field assignment (source_agent)\n- ✅ Project detection with ParentDir method\n\n### 6.2 What Needs Implementation\n\n- ⚠️ **First-line metadata extraction**: JSONL parser needs to read line 1, extract project field, and store in ParseContext\n- ⚠️ **Field-based project detection for JSONL**: Currently only implemented for JsonArray format, not Jsonl\n- ⚠️ **content[] array expansion**: Parser needs to expand content blocks into separate events (text → content, toolRequest → tool_call, toolResponse → tool_result)\n\n### 6.3 Current Workaround\n\nThe current goose plugin configuration uses:\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"field\"\nfield = \"working_dir\"\n```\n\nBut this relies on parser-level extraction that may not be fully implemented. A working alternative is:\n\n```toml\n[parser.project]\nmethod = \"parent_dir\"\n```\n\nThis uses the parent directory of the session file as the project path, which is less accurate but functional.\n\n## 7. Summary and Recommendations\n\n### 7.1 Schema Requirements\n\nThe goose plugin requires:\n1. **Line 1 handling**: Extract session metadata (`working_dir`, `description`, etc.)\n2. **Field-based project detection**: Implement `ProjectDetection::Field` for JSONL format\n3. **Content array expansion**: Parse `content[]` blocks into canonical events\n4. **Tool correlation**: Match `toolRequest.id` with `toolResponse.id`\n\n### 7.2 Project Detection Field Values\n\nFor goose, the project detection field is:\n- **Field name**: `working_dir`\n- **Location**: Line 1 (first line of JSONL file)\n- **Format**: Absolute file path string\n- **Example value**: `/home/coding/projects/my-app`\n\n### 7.3 Ready to Create Config\n\nWith this understanding, you can create a functional goose plugin configuration using:\n\n1. **Project detection**: `method = \"field\"` with `field = \"working_dir\"` (requires parser implementation) OR `method = \"parent_dir\"` (works immediately)\n2. **Timestamp field**: `timestamp = \"created\"` (Unix timestamp)\n3. **Role field**: `role = \"role\"` (user/assistant)\n4. **Content field**: `content = \"content\"` (array of blocks)\n\n### 7.4 Next Steps\n\nTo fully support goose with accurate project detection:\n\n1. Implement first-line metadata extraction in JSONL parser\n2. Implement content[] array expansion\n3. Add tool request/response correlation\n4. Test with real goose session files\n\nFor now, the plugin can function with `method = \"parent_dir\"` as a workaround until field-based detection is fully implemented for JSONL format.\n","numLines":419,"startLine":1,"totalLines":419}}}
